*Hifumi cooks us dinner when we return to Ayu's beach house, after a long day spent enjoying the azure surf and the golden sands.*
*She makes a bevy of traditional Japanese dishes, with mounds of perfectly-cooked, fluffy rice alongside it, which is all incredibly delicious.*
*Once the pans have been scoured, the dishes washed, and the cutlery put away, the sun has already set.*
*The moon is as round as one of the dinner plates, high in the night sky, and the stars twinkle serenely.*
*It's not as hot as it was during the day, but it's not cold, either.*
*It's a pleasant, picturesque kind of night.*
*It'd be nice to go for a walk on the beach after sundown...*
*But I'm stuffed after Hifumi's extravagant meal, and tired after canvassing the beach during the day.*
*I consider hitting the hay early, when a cheery proclamation from Hazel gives me pause.*
Hazel: "Hey, everyone, listen up! I just had a really great idea!"
Ayu: "Coming from you, that seems unlikely."

Hazel: "Ahem!"

Hazel: "I'm sure Hiroki here can confirm this for me!"

*Tired though I am, I can't possibly slink off to my room now. Everybody's waiting on me for an answer, and I don't want to let them down.*

Hazel: "It's time to step into your worst nightmare!"
*Once upon a time, not so very long ago, there was a cute but scatterbrained music student we'll call... hmm. How about Azusa?*
*Azusa was a cheerful girl, but she had a secret, which she nursed within her breast like a flickering flame.*
*She was passionately, irrevocably in love with the campus idol, Mamiko.*
*Mamiko was a beautiful girl from a wealthy family, with all the affectations of affluence.*
*She wore expensive clothes, sprayed herself with floral perfume, and her hair was neatly teased into curls.*
*She could play numerous instruments, her essays received the highest praise, and she was the darling of all the faculty - and most of the student body, too.*
*Azusa knew, when first she laid eyes upon Mamiko, that she had to have her for her own...*
*But their social standings were so disparate, Azusa feared Mamiko would never return her feelings!*
*Azusa was cast into the doldrums of blackest despair...*
*But, while she wallowed, a rumor began to spread around campus.*
*According to this rumor, one needed only to take a candid photograph of their beloved and text it to a mysterious woman called "Venus". Then, the pair would be bonded together, for all eternity!*
*Azusa did not believe in fairy tales - she was dense, but not wholly stupid - but was so desperate, she would have tried anything.*
*Sneakily, she took a photo of Mamiko, then sent it to the woman called "Venus"...*
*But, after that, things began to go horribly wrong.*
*It was late at night when Azusa's phone began to beep. The sound jolted her awake, to a bedroom darker than an abyss.*
*Eyes wide, her hair rumpled, Azusa reached for the phone. She took it betwixt her trembling fingers, heart pounding all the while, for she knew who it was who had messaged her.*
*It was Venus.*
*Azusa read through Venus's reply once, twice...*
*And then, she felt her heart constrict within her chest.*
*Venus's message read as thus... Ahem.*
*"Thank you, foolish girl, for sending me a photograph of your beloved. She truly is adorable, though I fear she shall not be for much longer."*
*"I am a fair witch, and I shall use my powers to bind you, as per the rumors. It would not do to go back upon my word - but alas! I will not let you get what you want that easily."*
*"Are you not familiar with the age-old saying, that one ought to be careful what they wish for?"*
*"If you truly loved this woman, you would confess to her on your own... but you did not. You were cowardly, and hoped instead to ensnare her with magic. This cannot go unpunished."*
*"My powers are great, and with it you shall get what you want, but not in the manner you envisioned."*
*"Mamiko's soul shall be mine, but her body is yours. You may do with it as you will, for all eternity, until it begins to rot - but she will not be the woman you remember."*
*"Her essence now belongs to me. She is naught but a husk... and she will haunt you for the rest of your days."*
*"Can you not hear her, foolish Azusa? She is coming to you this very moment, dragging her feet behind her. As I type, she is standing outside your home - and soon, she will begin to knock..."*
*As you may imagine, Azusa was terrified by this message.*
*She gripped her phone in her hands, curled up on her bed, and tried to console herself.*
*Surely it was some prank, done in bad taste. It was unthinkable that Mamiko - her Mamiko - could have been cursed, all thanks to a single photograph.*
*Azusa thought of texting her childhood friend, Mari, for reassurance, despite the lateness of the hour...*
*But no sooner than she had begun to scroll through her contacts list, she heard it.*
*A knock.*
*There was only one knock, at first, but it was low - ominous - and it startled Azusa so she dropped her phone upon her mattress.*
*Her heart constricted her chest, as though squeezed by a glacial hand, and her blood ran ice cold.*
*Perhaps the message had not been a joke after all. Venus had promised Mamiko would come to her, silent as a thief in the night, and rap upon her door - and here she was, as Venus had stated.*
*It was too precise to be a mere coincidence, and too late for Azusa to receive any other visitors.*
*The sky beyond her bedroom window was black as pitch. Even the stars and the moon seemed to have been extinguished.*
*There was no light in Azusa's room... nor any hope.*
*"Perhaps,", Azusa told herself, "if I sit very still and very quiet, the person outside my door will go away..."*
*But they did not.*
*Another knock rippled through the darkness of Azusa's home, unwinding in the air like thread.*
*Then, there came another, and another.*
*The knocking grew more insistent with the passing seconds, while Azusa grew more and more fearful.*
*Her heart was beating so hard, now, she feared it might rupture within her breast.*
*She did not want to answer the door...*
*But she feared, if she refused, the interloper would split it open like a walnut. Then, they would be even less obliged to listen to reason.*
*Nobody likes being kept out in the cold: not even monsters.*
*"Alright... I'm coming. I'll let you in, so please... don't hurt me. I never wanted this to happen..."*
*Azusa rose from her bed with trembling legs, then crossed her darkened bedroom. Her floorboards crrrrreaked beneath her weight, dark and sinister, and - all the while - the knocking continued.*
*It echoed within her ears, over and over, threatening to drive her to madness.*
*Azusa's apartment was a small one, with only two rooms. She had lived there for a year, and it was as familiar to her as her own palm... but in the dark, she stumbled.*
*Everything looked strange and shadowy, and even her rice cooker upon the counter made her start.*
*Her whole body was numb.*
*The knocking continued, over and over, until our intrepid - but stupid - young heroine reached the front door, where her shoes were lined up like Russian dolls.*
*She swallowed.*
*Then, she reached out.*
*"I'll let you in. I'll let you in, so please... Don't curse me. Don't hate me. I only did this because I love you. I'm so sorry, Mamiko..."*
*Azusa's fingers curled about the door handle. It was shockingly cool: so much so it seemed to drain the warmth from her skin.*
*She turned the door. The lock clicked, and it drew back...*
*To reveal a shambling figure that defied Azusa's comprehension.*
*It was Mamiko, but at the same token it was not.*
*Her hair, normally so perfect, fell in a tangle about her shoulders, obscuring much of her face. Her eyes were shrouded in darkness, but her mouth was twisted in a look of despair.*
*Her skin was white as chalk - white as death - and as she stumbled forth Azusa drew back, aghast, finally aware of what she had done.*
*Venus, whoever she was, had cast an awful enchantment upon her Mamiko.*
*She had, with nothing more than a photo, stripped all that was human from the prim and proper young lady, transfiguring her into something awful; something evil...*
*"No... Oh God, no..."*
*Azusa stumbled. She tripped, and hit the floor with a dull thud which could not break the pall of eerie silence.*
*"Mamiko... I-Is that really you...?"*
*Mamiko did not say anything. Perhaps she could not. She only drew closer, arms outstretched, as she made to fold Azusa to her.*
*To take her.*
*"Mamiko... I'm so sorry...! I'm so sorry..."*
*Azusa whimpered helplessly, tears beading in her eyes.*
*It seemed, as she sat there, staring at the thing which had once been Mamiko, that they were the only two people left in all the world.*
*She could scream, if she had the breath, but who would hear her?*
*Who would save her?*
*She had been plunged into a nightmare...*
*And she knew, as she felt Mamiko's fingers close about her wrist - her carrion breath, rasping and lifeless, upon her cheek - that she would never, ever wake up.*
Hazel: "So, whaddaya think?"
*Hazel concludes her tale with a self-satisfied grin, leaning back upon the couch with one leg crossed.*
Hazel: "That was pretty spooky, huh?"
Hifumi: "Well, I wonder..."

Hazel: "So, what were you sayin' about my story, Hifumi? D'you think it'd be better if there was more blood an' gore?"
Hifumi: "It's quite the opposite, in fact. I think you tipped too much of your hand."
Hifumi: "The way you built suspense was commendable, but it all fell apart when Mamiko was revealed to be the one at the door."
Hifumi: "If it were me, I would have ended the story before the big reveal. It is often better to keep the reader - or, in this case, the listener - guessing; particularly in horror."
Hifumi: "Often, our imaginations can conjure up scenarios more horrifying than words ever could."
Hazel: "So, you're not in the blood an' guts an' gore camp?"
Hifumi: "There is a time and a place for it... but it is not the sort of horror I prefer."
Hifumi: "I like subtly disquieting stories, with flickering lights and ambient noises. Ghosts and malcontent spirits are my forte: not blood-soaked zombies, or serial killers."
Hazel: "Hehehe. I guess I should've figured as much."
Hazel: "Lemme guess: you're a fan of creepy, abandoned shrines, too?"
Hifumi: "But of course! I am very partial to them!"
Hazel: "I knew it! And what about the rest of you? Did you like my story?"
*Hazel's looking at me expectantly, her eyes wide.*
*I guess she's waiting for feedback.*
*What should I say...?*
*I thought it was a good story.":
Hiroki: "I thought it was a pretty good story. It was very, uh, atmospheric."
Hiroki: "You're pretty good at building suspense, Hazel!"
Hazel: "Gee, d'you really think? That's so nice of you, Hiroki!"
Hazel: "This is why you're, like, the best boyfriend ever!"

Cosmos: "The story sounded kind of familiar..."

Ayu: "That's a crime, you know!"

Hazel: "Why not? I'm bad at thinkin' up names!"

Cosmos: "In the visual novel I read, Mamiko's soul wasn't stolen, and she didn't turn into a zombie."
Cosmos: "She was actually a sadist - kind of like Marina - and she bullied Azusa, but Azusa was a masochist, and she liked it, and they had a happy ending."
Cosmos: "There wasn't anything creepy about it."
Hazel: "There, y'see? It's totally different!"
Cosmos: "I don't know if you improved it, though..."
Cosmos: "I like stories with happy endings."
Hazel: "I take it you're not a fan of horror?"
Cosmos: "It's fine... but I want Azusa and Mamiko to be happy together."
Ayu: "I couldn't care one way or the other, personally. It's not like any of it is real. It doesn't really matter."

Hiroki: "Are you tired, Ayu?"

*Now, it's only Hazel, Marina, and I in the living room.*
Hazel: "So, what did you think?"

*Her exterior might be cold and hard, but deep down, Marina's a huge sweetheart.*
Hiroki: "Thanks, but I think I'll be fine. I've seen worse horror movies than that. Hazel's story really wasn't all that scary."
Marina: "I-It wasn't?"

Marina: "Now, um... I really must go. G-Goodnight, dear Hiroki."
Hiroki: "Yeah, uh... Goodnight."
*I watch as Marina departs on trembling legs, my arms folded.*
*Now I know it wasn't my imagination. Marina really was blushing - and she was stuttering, too.*
*This is all most un-Marina-like behavior.*
*I'm starting to get curious.*
*She isn't afraid of ghosts, is she...?*
Hiroki: "Haa... What a day..."
*I sigh to myself as I turn over beneath my covers.*
*It's late at night, and I'm exhausted, but I can't sleep.*
*My succubus entourage are running me ragged, what with their infighting and all their demands...*
*But I care for each of them, and I'm happy I was able to go on holiday with them.*
*It's been a lot of fun.*
*I hope we can spend even more time together tomorrow.*
Hiroki: "Aah..."
*I yawn again, and pull my covers about me like a cocoon.*
*Blanketed by the darkness, everything is still and quiet. I can hear, though faintly, the sound of the waves as they lap against the shore, but the seabirds are absent.*
*They must be fast asleep by now.*
*I feel should follow in their suit but, though I close my eyes, I still can't nod off.*
*I start counting sheep, hoping this will help, but...*
Hiroki: "Hmm?"
*I stall at my seventeenth sheep, distracted by a rapping of knuckles.*
*There's somebody at my bedroom door.*
*For a few brief moments, I think back on Hazel's horror story, and of the zombified Mamiko...*
???: "H-Hiroki? I'm sorry to disturb you but, um... I wanted to see you."
???: "Are you awake?"
*But the voice which accompanies the knocking is nothing at all like the guttural groaning of a bloodied zombie.*

Hiroki: "Marina?"
Marina: "Y-Yes, it's me. Please pardon my intrusion. Um..."
Marina: "Can I come in?"
Hiroki: "Sure you can - and don't worry. I hadn't fallen asleep yet anyway. You're not disturbing me."
Marina: "Thank goodness! I'm so glad!"
*Marina exhales in relief, then lets herself inside. She closes the door with a soft click, careful not to cause too much noise, then turns to face me.*
*Her bronzed skin seems to blend in with the darkness, but her ivory hair stands out; a flickering flame atop a tallow candle.*
*She isn't wearing her white sundress, and neither is she in her pajamas.*

Marina: "But as a human, I generally dispense with them, even at night."

Marina: "I-In case anything bad happens! If I'm in my succubus form, it'll be easier for me to fight off any enemies!"

Hiroki: "This is Ayu's private beach. We're the only people here. Nobody else is allowed to stay here; not without Ayu's express permission."
Hiroki: "The house is pretty well hidden, too..."
Hiroki: "It seems unlikely this place'd get broken into."

Marina: "I am more worried about..."

Hiroki: "What was that?"

Hiroki: "What are you worried about?"
Marina: "I-It is..."

Marina: "Silly. Very silly, in fact."
Marina: "I know I'm being foolish, but I can't help myself. I've been horribly on edge ever since I heard Hazel's silly story, though I am only too aware it is nonsensical!"
Marina: "I don't believe in ghosts or monsters, and yet... And yet..."

*I think it a perfectly sensible idea, but Marina doesn't seem to agree.*
Marina: "No, no, no! That will never do!"
Marina: "If I turn back now, it will ruin the fun for the others!"
Marina: "They sounded so enthused about this competition. As their older sister, I wouldn't want to let them down..."
Marina: "Moreover..."
*Marina clings to me even more tightly, if such a thing is possible, her brow furrowed with concern.*
Marina: "I wouldn't want them to realize that I'm afraid of monsters. I-It would ruin my credibility!"
Hiroki: "They wouldn't have to know. I could think of some excuse. Maybe I could say you sprained your ankle. Nobody would judge you then."
Marina: "A white lie may allow me to save face, but I would still know it to be a lie. I am an honorable woman, and I prefer to be honest and upfront."
Marina: "I do not wish to make my sisters worry about me, either."
Marina: "As their elder and their better, it is my job to fuss about them - not the other way around!"
Hiroki: "So, you're determined to see this though?"
Marina: "I must!"
Marina: "I cannot let my own fears get the better of me. I want to conquer my phobias once and for all."
Marina: "I have come so far, it would be a shame to retreat now..."
*She looks at me beneath a sweep of dark eyelashes, her face only partially illuminated by my flashlight.*
Marina: "And I know I will be safe with you. I trust you wholeheartedly."
Marina: "I am sure you will keep me safe, no matter what happens."
Hiroki: "Nothing's going to happen, Marina... but you're right on that score. I'll do my best."
Hiroki: "I'd be a pretty cruddy partner if I couldn't allay your worries."
Marina: "Thank you, dear. If I am with you, I should be able to tough this out. I just need to keep going."
Marina: "It shouldn't be that hard. I only need to place one foot after the other."
Marina: "I can do this."
*Marina's eyes burn with newfound determination, smouldering like fox fire.*
Marina: "I can."
Marina: "I have always been my own worst enemy. I shall not let my own fear best me. I am a mature woman, and I am far too old to get scared by things which go bump in the night."
Marina: "Really, I am being foolish. I know full well there's no such thing as ghosts or mon-"
Marina: "Kyaaaaa!!!"
*Marina breaks off, screaming, as the undergrowth rustles anew.*
*She clings to me, her head buried against my shoulder, and whimpers like a startled kitten.*
Marina: "I-I don't think I can take much more of this...!"
*...Well, so much for that burst of self-confidence. It vanished as quickly as it came: scattering on the breeze like cherry blossoms.*
*I guess Marina, headstrong though she is, isn't going to shake off her phobias any time soon.*
*The night stretches out ahead of me, dark and impenetrable.*
*At this rate, we'll never make it to the shrine.*
*I just hope we don't get lost. Then, I might start to lose my cool, too.*
*Half an hour later...*
Hiroki: "Well, whaddaya know. That wasn't half as hard as I'd feared."
*An old, dilapidated shrine looms before me in the darkness. Framed by trees, the shrine looks distinctly eerie, like something from a horror movie.*
*Its wooden door is padlocked shut, and old paper charms are stuck to it; either to keep malignant spirits out, or to keep them shut up inside.*
*It's clear from a glance the shrine hasn't been used in a long time. Some of the tiles have fallen off the roof, and its wooden supports are cracked.*
*The stone torches either side of the shrine are unlit, and the grass is overgrown; strewn with weeds and nettles.*
*I shine my flashlight over the edifice of the old building, picking out details in the gloom.*
*It's not a large shrine, but I wouldn't be surprised if it caved in under its own weight. It looks distinctly worm-eaten and woebegone.*
*When was the last time anybody came here to pray?*
*For all I know, it might have been ten, twenty - hell, even fifty - years.*
*I don't feel too perturbed as I gaze upon it. Instead, I feel a strange kind of sympathy.*
*There's something very sad and lonely about this rundown building.*
*I'm sure somebody must have cared for it once, in the past...*
*But the priest who tended it is, in all likelihood, too infirm to wade through the forest to unlock the door and sweep its old, stone steps.*
*That, or they're long dead.*
Hiroki: "It looks like we're the first ones here..."
*I shine the light about the dirt and the grass, but I can't see any footsteps. The place looks more or less untouched.*
*Hazel, Ayu, Hifumi, and Cosmos are conspicuously absent.*
*I guess it really didn't matter that Hazel had been to this shrine before. Her sense of direction is every bit as awful as she promised.*
Hiroki: "Does that make us the winners? I can't exactly kiss myself..."
*I turn to Marina, smiling.*
Hiroki: "...but you're more than welcome to claim your prize, if you want it. D'you want a celebratory kiss?"
Marina: "..."
*Marina doesn't respond. She stands a few feet away from me, hands clenched at her front, her eyes wide - too wide - as she stares at the shrine.*
*She didn't say all that much during our trek through the woods, but now she's completely silent.*
*She's standing as still and stiff as the posts which support the shrine.*
*Were it not for the billowing of her white sundress, which flutters about her legs, I'd worry she'd turned into stone.*
Hiroki: "Marina...? Are you OK?"
Marina: "..."
*Still, she says nothing. She looks utterly dumbfounded.*
Hiroki: "I guess this place is pretty scary... but don't worry. There's nothing to be worried about."
Hiroki: "There aren't any ghosts here - and even if there are, I'll fight them off. You've got absolutely nothing to be scared abou-"
*I try to reassure her, but my voice is cut off by a rustle from the undergrowth - far louder than that caused by the scampering rabbits.*
*A branch cracks, loud as a gunshot, then another.*
*It sounds like footsteps.*
*Something is approaching us through the copse of trees: something shadowy and unseen, cloaked in the night.*
???: "Hiroki!"
*A disembodied voice cries, cutting through the stillness of the night.*
???: "Hiroki, is that you? Please, say it is. I've been stumbling around in the woods for hours."
???: "It's so cold..."
???: "I need you to warm me up!"
*A bipedal figure is upon us, with pale skin and dark streaming hair that seems to blot out the moonlight.*
*I see two wide eyes, powder blue, flickering behind long lashes like will-o'-wisps...*
*Then, the orange light emitting from my flashlight begins to gutter. It flickers, then fades, plunging both Marina and I into near-perfect darkness.*
Marina: "N-No... Please, stay back..."
Hiroki: "Marina, calm down-"
Marina: "No, don't hurt me...!"
Hiroki: "Marina...!"
Marina: "KYAAAAAAAA!!!!!"
*A blood-curdling scream wrenches itself from Marina's throat.*
*Eyes squeezed shut, she sinks down onto the ground, her hands clapped firmly over her ears.*
*It looks like she's having some sort of breakdown.*
*It sounds like she's having a breakdown, too.*
*I've never seen Marina cry like that before...*
*And neither, it seems, has our mysterious interloper with the pale blue eyes.*
???: "Dear me... Did I do something to upset her?"
Hiroki: "No, uh... she was pretty jumpy already. The flashlight going off must've been the last straw."
Hiroki: "You didn't do anything wrong, Yue."
Yue: "Thank goodness. That is a relief..."
*The shadowy figure - or, should I say, Yue - sighs, in tandem with the flickering of my flashlight.*
*The orange light emits from it anew, to reveal the countenance of a woman I know very well.*
*Yue stands before me, framed by a silhouetted row of dark trees.*
*Her long, black hair - which, now that I think about it, does bear a striking resemblance to that of a certain TV-dwelling ghost - flutters around her pale face, framing it.*
*She's wearing, not her succubus attire, nor her extravagant "casual clothes" (if they can be called that), but a blue and white bikini.*
*It's a very cute swimsuit, with girlish ruffles, but it doesn't really suit the environment.*
Hiroki: "Why are you dressed like that... and what are you doing here?"
Yue: "Oh, that, um..."
*Yue glances away, uncharacteristically flustered, and presses the tips of her index fingers together.*
*A pale pink blush captures her cheeks, and her eyelashes qiver.*
Yue: "It's a long story. You see, er..."
Yue: "I-I heard you were all going on a summer vacation, a-and I thought it might be fun to join you."
Yue: "I didn't want to be left out."
*Well, that's fair. I don't think anybody enjoys being excluded, unless you're a particular type of masochist.*
Hiroki: "Sorry about that. I would've invited you along, if I'd known you'd wanted to come, but..."
Hiroki: "Wait."
*I pause, my brow furrowed.*
Hiroki: "I thought Ayu did invite you, and you refused."
Hiroki: "Didn't you say you were too busy running the succubus realm to take time off?"
Yue: "Yes, indeed. I was busy: horribly busy. I still am, in fact."
Yue: "My succubi are so demanding. They cannot be without me for too long, else everything goes - quite literally - to Hell."
Yue: "My subjects are very sweet, but they need to be disciplined. As their princess, I cannot let anarchy take sway!"
Hiroki: "I get that. It sounds like you have your work cut out for you."
Yue: "It is a lot of work. That is why I rejected Ayu's initial offer..."
Yue: "But, after giving it some thought, I decided it would not be so very bad if I took a small break."
Yue: "Time passes differently in the succubus realm, as you should be only too aware. A full day here, beneath the sun..."
*She pauses.*
Yue: "Not that it is shining right now, but that is beside the point."
Yue: "A full day here lasts only a handful of hours in the succubus realm."
Hiroki: "Yeah, I am aware..."
*Yue trapped me in the succubus realm for weeks and weeks not so long ago.*
*When I finally escaped - in no small part thanks to Ayu - I was relieved to discover only a couple of days had gone by in the human world.*
*I only missed a bit of work, and I didn't miss any of my rent payments for my cruddy apartment.*
*That was a huge load off my mind.*
Yue: "I want to be a proper, professional ruler, who always puts the needs of my subjects first, but I will hardly be a good princess if I am perpetually tired!"
Yue: "That is how I decided to justify - I mean, ahem..."
*Yue clears her throat.*
Yue: "That is how I came to the objective, not at all biased decision that I would like - no, that I need - a break."
Yue: "This is for the sake of my subjects."
Yue: "I'm not abandoning them for the pursuit of my own leisure, so please, do not get the wrong idea!"
Yue: "I want to have fun with the rest of you, yes..."
Yue: "But I am actually being very selfless!"
Yue: "I am here for the good of all the denizens of my realm, and therefore, nobody can criticize me!"
*That's what Yue says, but she doesn't look at all certain. Her face is still red (with guilt, I think) and she's unable to meet my gaze.*
*Is she afraid that I'm judging her?*
*I am looking at her with some scrutiny, it's true, but that's not because of her supposed carelessness.*
*She looks pretty good in that bikini...*
*But she still hasn't answered my earlier question.*
Hiroki: "Alright. I get why you decided to come here now - and, to be honest, I don't blame you."
Hiroki: "You've not done anything to be ashamed of..."
Hiroki: "But, if you wanted to visit us at the beach house, you should have done just that."
Hiroki: "I don't know what you're doing in this forest, or why you've been wandering around wearing a swimsuit."
Hiroki: "You almost gave Marina a heart attack when you stumbled out the forest like that!"
*I glance at Marina, as does Yue. She's still crouched on the ground, arms wound around her legs.*
*She's rocking back and forth, muttering to herself.*
*I can't hear what she's saying exactly, but it sounds vaguely like the heart sutra.*
*Is she trying to calm herself down...?*
*Yue might not be a ghost, but does does look pretty spectral, with her pale skin and her long, dark hair.*
*Her sudden, unexpected appearance must have given Marina quite the scare.*
Yue: "Well, um... In regards to that..."
*Yue's face turns redder still.*
*The lukewarm breeze stirs her long hair, so it swishes about her.*
Yue: "I left the succubus realm a couple of hours ago in your time... which would have been a day or so in mine."
Yue: "I meant to visit you at your beach house, wearing the brand new swimsuit I picked out, but..."
*She dips her head.*
Yue: "I was so excited, my coordinates were a bit off when I teleported."
Yue: "Rather than appear at Ayu's doorstep, which I intended, I instead ended up in the middle of this forest."
Yue: "I have been trying to find my way out ever since, b-but I'm not very good at directions..."
Yue: "It feels like I've been going around in circles!"
Yue: "I was afraid I would succumb to exhaustion and collapse, to be feasted upon by wild wolves, until you appeared."
Yue: "You saved me, Hiroki! You are my prince!"
Hiroki: "Gee, uh... Don't mention it."
Hiroki: "It sounds like you've had quite a hard time?"
Yue: "Yes, indeed I have! This is not how I imagined my holiday going - not at all! I've spent hours in this stupid forest..."
*Yue's stomach rumbles. Embarrassed, she whimpers.*
Yue: "...and I'm so, so hungry!"
Hiroki: "Haven't you eaten in a day?"
*Yue shakes her head pitifully.*
Yue: "I did find some funny-looking mushrooms growing by the base of a tree. I was so desperate, I thought about eating them-"
*Yeah, that doesn't sound like the best idea.*
Yue: "-but I reconsidered. They were red, with white spots, and they didn't smell good."
Yue: "I was afraid they were poisonous."
Hiroki: "I'd say you dodged a bullet there, though now I'm wondering. Can you poison a succubus?"
Yue: "Of course you can. We are not so different from humans, though we are a bit tougher."
Yue: "Poisons which might be lethal to a human would not be so for me. They would only make my stomach cramp."
Yue: "I would not wish to spend my holiday vomiting, though. That would take all the fun out of coming here!"
Hiroki: "I imagine it would."
Hiroki: "Well..."
*I offer Yue a small smile.*
Hiroki: "I'm glad I found you - but you hardly needed to wait for me to rescue you."
Yue: "What do you mean?"
Hiroki: "You're a succubus, right? When you transform, you have wings."
Yue: "Yes, and...?"
Hiroki: "Er..."
*If it weren't for Yue's very earnest expression, I'd think she was playing dumb on purpose.*
*This solution to her problem seems so obvious to me - and I don't even have wings - I'm baffled she never hit upon it during her sojourn in the forest, meandering among the trees while slowly starving.*
*I've always thought Yue a sensible person...*
*But I guess even the best of us have blind spots.*
Hiroki: "If you weren't getting anywhere on foot, you could have flown. There was nothing stopping you flying out of here, was there?"
Yue: "...!"
*Yue's eyes widen.*
*Did she seriously not think of this before? I can hardly believe it.*
Yue: "I... I, um... Uh..."
*Yue opens and closes her mouth, shame-faced, searching for a reply, when...*
???: "Alright! Looks like we're nearly there!"
*I hear footsteps, coupled with a loud, cheery voice. It sounds a lot like Hazel.*
*She must've found her way here, at long last, though it took a while.*
Hiroki: "It seems like we have company. D'you want to greet them, Yue?"
*I turn to Yue, but she doesn't hang around. Instead, she scrambles away; secreting herself away behind the dilapidated shrine.*
*Why's she trying to hide? Is she that embarrassed about getting lost?*
*I guess I can't blame her on that front, but she can't hide the truth forever!*
*As the succubus princess, she needs to learn to 'fess up for her mistakes!*
Hiroki: "Hey, Yue-"
*I hiss at her, but I'm cut off by Hazel. Sporty Succubus descends upon me, giving me a congratulatory smack on the back.*
Hazel: "You must've been pretty speedy, to get here so soon! Congrats!"
Cosmos: "Urgh... That isn't fair."
Cosmos: "I was determined to get here first, so I could steal your lips, but you beat me."
Cosmos: "Now, you will have to kiss yourself."
Cosmos: "I wonder how that will work?"
Hazel: "With a mirror! I'm sure Hiroki can always make out with his own reflection, ahaha!"
Cosmos: "Ah yes. I did not think about that. How ingenious."
Cosmos: "I suppose it is possible to kiss yourself after all..."
Cosmos: "But wouldn't that be self-cest? BL self-cest?"
Cosmos: "That's so lewd..."
Ayu: "What're you jabbering on about? Did you guys get here before us?"
Cosmos: "Oh, Ayu! Good timing! I was just thinking about BL self-cest!"
Ayu: "Huh? What's that?"
Cosmos: "Please, allow me to explain."
Cosmos: "When a man loves another man very much - who, in this case, just so happens to be himself - they get together to do naughty things, and then they lay an egg!"
Hifumi: "What sort of egg? A soft-boiled one?"
Cosmos: "Um..."
*Cosmos ponders, one hand beneath her chin.*
Cosmos: "I'm not sure about the specifics. I just read about it once, in a doujin. It looked very convincing."
Ayu: "Yeah, sure."
*Ayu rolls her eyes.*
Ayu: "You need to get some better hobbies, Cosmos. Reading all that junk is rotting your brain."
Cosmos: "You say that, but some doujins have really cute art and really moving stories! The people who make them put a whole buncha effort into them!"
Cosmos: "I love reading them - even the steamy ones!"
Cosmos: "I've even found a few doujins of you, Ayu!"
Ayu: "Please, don't talk to me about that. I don't even want to imagine the delusional fantasies about me that are available online."
Ayu: "I only have so much faith in humanity."
Hifumi: "Now, now, Ayu. It's not nice to be misanthropic."
*Hifumi pats Ayu on the head, as she is wont to doing.*
Hifumi: "You ought to try and see the best in others. Obsessing over the negatives will only upset you!"
Cosmos: "Hifumin's right! You've gotta look on the bright side! All those doujin authors did, when they wrote about you!"
Cosmos: "They make you way, way nicer'n you are in real life..."
Cosmos: "And they make your chest bigger, too!"
Ayu: "H-How dare you?! Th-There's nothing wrong with my chest!!!"
Hazel: "So, I see you're in one piece..."
*Hazel looks me up and down, smiling.*
Hazel: "But why's Marina all hunched up in a pile over there?"
Hazel: "She hasn't hurt herself, has she?"
Hiroki: "She should be fine. She just had a bit of a fright..."
Hazel: "What sorta fright? She didn't see a real ghost, did she?"
Hifumi: "Goodness me! I would love to see a real ghost! Could you tell me all about it, Marina?"
Hifumi: "What did it look like? Was its skin very white? Was it wearing a funereal kimono?"
Hifumi: "Perhaps it was crying tears of blood, or else murmuring about revenge?"
Hiroki: "Not quite, no. This ghost was more corporeal than that. You see..."
*I begin, but I'm cut off by a confident voice, full of pomp.*
Yue: "Yes, it is I: Princess Yue of the succubus realm. I have deigned to visit you, my foolish subordinates, so that you might bask in my splendor."
Yue: "Rejoice, for now I am here to watch over you!"
*Yue steps out from behind the ramshackle shrine, her appearance drastically altered.*
*She was wearing a skimpy bikini when I ran into her, but now she's in her succubus regalia: her horns proud and tall, her shoulders enveloped by a fur cloak.*
*There's no trace of her prior embarrassment upon her alabaster face. Her pale blue eyes are hard and commanding.*
*She looks cool and composed, as she did upon her throne in the succubus realm.*
*I'm aware she's acting, but damn, it's a good act. She could even give Hifumi a run for her money!*
Hazel: "Wow! Lady Yue! I didn't think you'd come here!"
Ayu: "I was hoping you wouldn't come here. I don't want you breathing down my neck for the rest of the holiday!"
Yue: "Hmph."
*Yue turns her nose up in the air.*
Yue: "You need not worry on that score, Ayu. I have no desire to trouble myself with the likes of you."
Yue: "I came here only so I could recuperate, so I might better rule my subjects in the succubus realm."
Yue: "I do not care for you at all."
Ayu: "Oh yeah? W-Well, same here! It's not like I'm grateful you let Hiroki go from the succubus realm back then or anything, y-you idiot!"
Hifumi: "I, for one, think this is quite the fortuitous turn of events. It is very good to see you, Yue! How long have you been here?"
Yue: "Oh, uh, about that..."
*Yue's façade splinters for a fraction of a second. Abashed, she looks away, her cheeks pink.*
Yue: "I-I only just got here. I was drawn, you see, by the sounds of your merrymaking. I was attracted, like a moth to the flame, and then..."
*Yue's words trail away, and her stomach growls.*
Yue: "...!"
*The rumbling is so loud, even Marina ceases her shaking.*
*She gets to her feet, though her legs are unsteady, and regards Yue with some surprise.*
Marina: "When did you get here?"
Hifumi: "That is what I am trying to ascertain. She said she only just arrived, but based upon her guilty expression, I would wager it has been longer than that."
Hifumi: "You did not get lost, did you?"
Yue: "N-No, I didn't! Really, I didn't!"
Hifumi: "Are you sure? I know your sense of direction is somewhat lacking - though you are very competent in all other regards."
Hifumi: "You needn't be embarrassed, dear. I am your older cousin, and I know your foibles better than anybody."
Hifumi: "Please, do not feel the need to hide your secrets from me!"
Yue: "I-I'm not worried about you so much as I am the others..."
*Yue glares at Ayu and Marina.*
Yue: "B-But, well, um... I-If that is what you wish to believe, i-it is not so very far from the truth."
Yue: "I have been here for a while, and I am rather hungry."
Yue: "You, um... wouldn't mind preparing some food for me when we return, would you, Hifumi?"
Hifumi: "Of course not. It would be my pleasure!"
Hifumi: "Now, why don't we retrace our footsteps? I think we've had more than enough excitement for one night!"
*Hifumi has a very good point there. So much has happened, I'm still reeling from it.*
*I can't wait to get back home and have a lie down.*
*I think I've earned it.*
*After the excitement at the abandoned shrine, our party returns to Ayu's beach house with our plus one.*
*Yue tags along behind us like a petulant child, her head hanging.*
*Hifumi whips Yue up something to eat when we return, which Yue falls upon ravenously.*
*Her hunger satiated, she then pays me a visit in my room.*
*Yue is no longer attired in her skintight succubus outfit, nor her bikini. She's clad, instead, in a sweet, almost demure white dress with a blue sash tied about the front.*
*The sash is almost the same color as her eyes, and her dark hair contrasts nicely with the pale fabric of her dress.*
*I let Yue into my room, and she takes a seat upon the side of my bed.*
*Several times, she opens her mouth, as though she means to say something... but, in the end, she's unable to get any words out.*
*She's acting very strangely (though not, I note with some relief, as strangely as she acted when I first found her).*
*Something must be amiss. I wonder what that could be.*
Hiroki: "Are you alright, Yue?"
Yue: "Yes, I'm fine, thank you for asking."
Yue: "I should probably away to my own room - Ayu said she would prepare one for me - but I thought, at first, I would like to speak to you."
Yue: "I hope it is no intrusion?"
*Yue isn't acting at all how I'm accustomed to. In the succubus realm, she was demanding - forceful, even.*
*Back then, she had no qualms in exerting her dominance over others.*
*Now, however, she seems the complete opposite.*
*Perhaps that's because we're in the human world, not the succubus realm. She's pretending to be an ordinary person, not a princess, and she's been stripped of much of her pomp.*
*She was pretty awkward when I first met her a decade ago, when she was masquerading as an ordinary high school student called Yukie.*
*She didn't have many (or any) friends, and she never spoke to anybody apart from me.*
*I thought she was aloof, but I might have been mistaken.*
*It's possible she was simply shy.*
Hiroki: "It's not an intrusion at all. I'm glad I got a chance to see you again."
Yue: "Really...?"
Hiroki: "Yes, really! It wouldn't be much of a holiday if I couldn't spend it with all my girlfriends."
Hiroki: "I like the others an awful lot... but you're special to me too, Yue."
Hiroki: "You were my very first girlfriend, and I still cherish you."
Yue: "Even though I was so thoughtlessly possessive?"
Hiroki: "Even so. We've moved past that. It's water under the bridge."
Hiroki: "I understand you were acting out 'cause of jealousy, and you since apologized."
Hiroki: "I'm not going to hold it against you."
Yue: "That is a relief. It has been such a long time since I last saw you - I have been awfully busy with work! - I began to worry."
Yue: "In truth, I wanted to come on vacation with you very much..."
Yue: "But it was not just my sense of duty which deterred me."
Yue: "I was afraid, if I came, I would spoil things for you."
Yue: "I behaved very selfishly when I took you to the succubus realm. I took you against your will, and held you captive."
Yue: "I dominated your every waking hour, and tried to keep you away from the others."
Yue: "It was wrong of me."
Yue: "I would not usually act in so rash a manner - particularly not for the sake of a human - but I cannot think clearly when I am with you."
Yue: "My judgment evaporates, as does my sense of right and wrong."
Yue: "I still love you, after all these years..."
Yue: "But that does not excuse what I did."
Yue: "I wished to punish myself, I suppose, by keeping away from you, and hiding away in the succubus realm..."
Yue: "But I was curious, and I found myself watching you and the others from afar."
Yue: "You looked like you were having so much fun, I could no longer stand it."
Yue: "My resolve crumbled, and I came to see you..."
Yue: "But I was so hasty, I made a grave error. I got lost, then made a fool out of myself."
Yue: "You must think me so stupid!"
Hiroki: "Stupid, no. Silly, well... Maybe."
Hiroki: "It's nothing to beat yourself up over, though. We all make mistakes..."
Hiroki: "And, like I said, I'm glad you decided to stop by. It felt like something was missing without you."
Hiroki: "Don't think you need to stay away from me. I don't want you punishing yourself, or making yourself unhappy."
Hiroki: "I far prefer seeing you smile."
Yue: "Oh, Hiroki... You're so cool..."
Yue: "I have met other human men in my time, but none stir my heart as you do."
Yue: "This is why I fell for you - and I believe I may be falling still. Every time we meet, I find myself loving you even more."
Yue: "I missed you so, so much..."
Hiroki: "I missed you, too."
*Yue leans in. Our faces are inches apart, our lips almost brushing, when-*
Hazel: "Lady Yue! It's been so long! I'm so glad to see you again!!!"
*Hazel comes bounding into our room, a flurry of excitable, puppyish energy.*
*She was wearing her casual clothes when she threw my door open, but her appearance shifts like rippling water upon seeing Yue.*
*Horns sprout from her head, and a tail protrudes from her back.*

*Yue was my first ever girlfriend, and while she's no longer my only girlfriend, she still holds a special place in my heart.*
